NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
TUESDAY, June 12, 2012   
 
PRESENT:  David Tenney, Supervisor; Jesse Thompson, Supervisor; Jonathan M. Nez, Supervisor; and Jerry 
Brownlow Vice-Chairman were present. Supervisor Brownlow indicated that Chairman DeSpain will be present 
momentarily.  
9:04 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation led by Supervisor Tenney. 
CALL TO THE PUBLIC: There were no public comments.  
CONSENT AGENDA:  a)Constable Report(s): Winslow, Precinct #2, April 2012 ; Snowflake, Precinct #3, 
May 2012; Show Low, Precinct #5, May 2012  b)Justice Court Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, May; Winslow, 
Precinct #2, May; Snowflake Precinct #3, May;  Kayenta Precinct #4, May; Show Low Precinct #5, May; 
Pinetop-Lakeside Precinct #6, May  c)Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000 May, 2012  d)Conduct precinct-
based elect ion for Show Low Fire District on November 6, 2012 to elect 3 positions with a 4 year term 
beginning December 1, 20 12 and ending November 30, 2016  e)Conduct precinct -based election for Clay 
Springs Domestic Water Improvement District on November 6 to elect  2 positions with a 4 year term beginning 
January 1, 2013 and ending December 31, 2016  f)Conduct precinct-based election for Lakeside Fire District on 
November 6, 2012 to elect 3 positions with a 4 year term beginning December 1, 20 12 and ending November 
30, 2016  g)Conduct a precinct -based election for Sun Valley Fire District on November 6, 2012 to elect 1 
position with a 4 year term beginning January 1, 20 13 and ending December 31, 2016  h)Conduct a precinct -
based election for Heber -Overgaard Fire District  on November 6, 2012 to elect 2 positions with a 4 year term 
beginning December 1, 2 012 and ending November 6, 2016  i)Conduct a precinct-based election for Ponderosa 
Domestic Water Improvement District on November 6, 2012 to elect 3 positions with a 4 year  term beginning 
January 1, 2013 and ending December 31, 2016  j)Conduct a precinct -based election for Wonderland Acres 
Domestic Water Improvement District on November 6, 2012 to elect 2 positions with a 4 year term beginning 
January 1, 2 013 and ending Decem ber 31, 2016  k)Rename an existing road in the Shumway area to “Mission 
Hill Road” in Section 30, Township 12 North, Range 22 East l)Approval of the two year term for Max Taylor to 
serve on the Navajo County Extension Advisory Board to be retroactive on Apr il 1, 2012 m)Reappointment of 
Bill Rawlings to the Planning & Zoning Commission for District V until January 31, 2015  n)Reappointment of 
Wendell DeCross to the Planning & Zoning Commission for District V until January 31, 2014 o)Reappointment 
of Jason Hatch to the Planning & Zoning Commission for District IV until January 31, 2014  p)Reappointment 
of Joel Lawson to the Planning & Zoning Commission for District III until January 31, 2015  q)Reappointment 
of Bob Hall to the Planning & Zoning Commission for District III until January  31, 2014 r)Reappointment of 
Fred Shupla to the Planning & Zoning Commission for District II until January 31, 2014  s)Reappointment of 
Ruth Ann Smith to the Planning & Zoning Commission for District IV until January 31, 2015  t)Approve the 
revised Amendment to the Revolving Credit Agreement  u)Agreement between Arizona State Forestry Division 
and Navajo County Board of Supervisors regarding E nvironmental Program Assistance  v)Approval of the use 
of the southwest corner of the Navajo County Government Complex for the annual 4th of July Fireworks display 
w)Approval of Third Amended Intergovernmental Agreement with Arizona Counties Insurance Pool (ACIP)  
x)Sympathy letter for Sheriff Clark : Supervisor Tenney made a motion  to approve the co nsent agenda as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 06-12-2012 
Page 2 of 4 
 
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  
a. RSC Rental Corporation and Navajo County Recognizing Excellence : Dale Nielson recognized LaRee 
Price for her excellence. Mr. Nielson stated that LaRee put together a grant with the help of the Grant 
Task Force. He further stated that she has  put in countless hours well beyond the work day and that s he 
is an exceptional employee. Laree thanked the Board and expressed her gratitude.  
b. Personnel Service Awards : David Coolidge recognized the following employees for their years of 
service to Navajo County: Cris Parisot, five years; Lynda Sonin , five years; Lori Turley , five years; 
Joseph Kay, five years; Shelly Hill, five years; Seth Morris, five years; Jason Van Aller, five years; Jerry 
Sowle, five years; Louann Scoggin, 10 years; Dalton Quimby, 10 years; and Mary Tyler, 35 years.   
HUMAN RESOURCES: Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions : David Coolidge  
introduced Patrick  Shinn the new Human Resou rces Technician. Mr. Coolidge further reviewed the 28 item 
personnel action report. Supervisor Tenney made a motion  to authorize the Vice Chairman to sign the 
Personnel Action Report as presented. Supervisor Tenney indicated that he has a conflict of interest on item 
number 23. The motion was seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
Chairman DeSpain is present at 9:19 a.m. 
ADMINISTRATION: Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution N umber 13-12 adopting a 
Residential Anti-Displacement and Relocation Assistance Plan as required under section 104(D) of the Housing 
and Community Development Act of 1974 as amended : Bill Chaddick discussed the item . Supervisor Tenney 
made a motion to adopt Resolution Number 13-12, adopting a Residential Anit-Displacement and Relocation 
Assistance Plan as required under section 104(D) of the Housing and Community Development Act of 1974 as 
amended; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.   
NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT Board of Directors Session : Supervisor 
Tenney made a motion to enter into the Navajo County Public Health Services District Board of Directors 
Session; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson . Vote unanimous approving the motion.  CONSENT 
AGENDA: i) Consideration and possible approval of Contract #GRA -State-13-0505-01 with the Arizona Early 
Childhood Development and Health Board for a Child Care Health Consultant Program for Navajo and Apache 
Counties for $6,34 5.00 from July 1, 2012 to June 30, 2013 ii) Consideration and possible approval of IGA 
Amendment #3 of Contract #HG060014 for Tobacco Education and Prevention with the Arizona Department of 
Health Services for July 1, 2012 to June 30, 2013 in the amount of  $180,000.00 iii) Consideration and possible 
approval of the Intergovernmental Agreement with White Cone Chapter to partner on the use of the Multi -Use 
Facility in Wh ite Cone : Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve the consent agenda as presented; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.   Supervisor Tenney made a 
motion to return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving 
the motion.  
COUNTY ATTORNEY: Consideration and possible approval of the Crime Victim Assistance Program ACJC 
Grant Number VA-026: Alisa Schiebler discussed the item. Supervisor Thompson made a motion to approve 
the Crime Victim Assistance Program ACJC Grant Number VA -026; motion seco nded by Supervisor 
Brownlow. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
JUVENILE PROBATION:  Consideration and possible approval of Family Counseling Funding County 
Match of $4,296 to the Juvenile Probation Department so they may receive the Family Counseling Gr ant from 
the Administrative Office of the Courts of $17, 182 for a total of $21,478 for the 12/13 Fiscal Year :  Arno Hall

<!-- page 3 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 06-12-2012 
Page 3 of 4 
 
discussed the item. Supervisor Thompson made a motion to approve the Family Counseling Funding County 
Match of $4,296 to the Juvenile Probation Department so they may receive the Family Counseling Grant from 
the Administrative Office of the Courts of $17, 182 for a total of $21,478 for the 12/13 Fiscal Year ; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
ELECTIONS: Consideration and p ossible approval  of Resolution Number 14-12 amending the Navajo 
County voting precinct map and polling places pursuant to A.R.S. §16-411(B): Johnathan Roes discussed the 
item and answered questions of the Board . Supervisor Tenney made a motion to adopt Resolution Number 
14-12, amending the Navajo County voting precinct map and polling places pursuant to A.R.S. §16 -411 (B); 
motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Discussion was held. Mr. Roes addressed the concern s of the 
Board. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
FINANCE:  
a. Consideration and possible approval of the award contract amendment for Phase II “A” F11 -01-04 for 
Emergency Regional Communications Study to iXP  Corporation in the amount of $5,000 for Phase 2B 
additional workshop : Mary Springer discussed the item. Supervisor Brownlow made a motion to 
award contract amendment for Phase II “A” F11-01-04 for Emergency Regional Communications Study 
to iXP Corporation  in the amount of $5,000 for Phase 2B additional workshop ; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of the award contract F12 -05-07 for annual newspaper printing and 
advertising bid to Holbrook Tribune News : Mary Springer discussed the item to be gin July 1, 2012. 
Supervisor Brownlow made a motion to award contract F12-05-07 for annual newspaper printing and 
advertising bid to Holbrook Tribune News ; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez . Vote  unanimous 
approving the motion.  
c. Consideration and possible approval of a five year lease agreement with the White Mountain 
Community Garden for the purposes of planting a community garden, providing educational activities, 
workshops and fundraising events related to gardening and a farmer’s market : Mary Springer discussed 
the item and introduced Sandy Russell. Sandy Russell and members of the Community Garden gave a 
brief presentation. Supervisor Brownlow thanked the Community G arden, Jason Moore and Jimm y 
Jayne. Supervisor Brownlow made a motion  to approve the five year lease agreement with the White 
Mountain Community Garden for the purposes of planting a community garden, providing educational 
activities, workshops and fundraising events related to gardening and a farmer’s market ; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
COUNTY SUPERVISORS ASSOCIATION: County Update:  Craig Sullivan gave a brief presentation on the 
County Supervisors Association. Mr. Sullivan thanke d Supervisor Tenney and Hunter Moore for their work 
with the Legislature. Supervisor Tenney thanked Craig and his team for what they have done for Navajo 
County. Supervisor Thompson expressed his gratitude.  Recorder Laurette Justman and Assessor Cammy 
Darris thanked CSA for their support of Navajo County and the partnership with AACO. James Jayne thanked 
Craig Sulllivan for his work and the partnerships. Chairman DeSpain thanked Craig for his efforts and 
teamwork with Navajo County and AACO.  
PUBLIC WORKS: 
a. Consideration and possible approval of Resolution Number 15-12 to enter into an Intergovernmental 
Agreement ( IGA) with the State of Arizona, Department of Transportation, for the replacement of 
regulatory, warning and street names signs under the Highway S afety Improvement Program (HSIP) 
funding: Homero Vela discussed the item. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to adopt Resolution

<!-- page 4 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 06-12-2012 
Page 4 of 4 
 
Number 15-12 approving the Intergovernmental Agreement with the State of Arizona, Department of 
Transportation, for the replacement of regulatory, warning and street names signs under the Highway 
Safety Improvement Program funding; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving 
the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of agreement revisions to RAC Grant  award and authorize the 
Chairman to sign the revised agreement: Homero Vela discussed the item. Supervisor Brownlow made 
a motion to approve the agreement revision to RAC Grant award and authorize the Chairman to sign the 
revised agreement; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
c. Presentation on Freeman Hollow Road and Pinedale Road double chip seals and the Navajo County 
Memorial Day Float: Homero Vela discussed the item and gave a brief presentation. Mr. Vela thanked 
his team and Bryan Cook for his presentation. Jimmy thanked Bryan Cook and Homero Vela.  
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE: Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager : 
Supervisor Tenney  reported on meeting s scheduled with Senator Biggs  and some legi slative impacts. 
Supervisor Thompson gave a report from District II  including meetings in White Cone . Supervisor Nez gave 
a report from District I. Supervisor Brownlow gave a report from District V ; he spoke of the meeting with the 
Tribal Council regarding senior center funding. He also spoke about the new mayor of Show Low and Pinetop 
Lakeside. He further thanked Craig Sullivan and his team for all their hard work on the many issues facing 
Navajo County and all our other partners. James Jayne thanked Catri na for the dessert today; he also thanked 
the public works team for the overlay projects.   
ADJOURN: At 10:53 a.m. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to adjourn; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Brownlow. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
 
APPROVED: 
 
__________________________________ 
J.R. DeSpain, Chairman 
 
ATTEST: 
 
DATE: July 24, 2012 
 
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board
